# 01. Facebook (Meta) - Chapter and Pattern Wise

[Back to Company Wise Questions](../README.md) · [Back to DSA Notes](../../README.md)

Source: LeetCode company lists for Meta (Premium), pulled on 2026-11-27.

1379 unique problems: 437 Easy, 737 Medium, 205 Hard. Each problem appears exactly once.

Window sizes: Last 30 days = 9, Last 3 months = 113, Last 6 months = 318, More than 6 months = 1231.

Chapters go from basic to advanced. Each problem sits in the sub-pattern of its standard optimal solution.

Folder layout: `NN. Chapter/NN. Sub-pattern/NNNN. Problem/NNNN. Problem.md`

## Roadmap

- **[01. Arrays & Hashing](01.%20Arrays%20%26%20Hashing/README.md)** (117): Array Traversal & Basics (32), In-Place Array Manipulation (4), Hash Map Lookup (12), Hash Set & Duplicates (21), Frequency Counting (33), Grouping by Key (4), Kadane's Algorithm (4), Cyclic Sort & Index Marking (5), Majority Element (Boyer-Moore) (2)
- **[02. Strings](02.%20Strings/README.md)** (65): String Basics & Manipulation (17), Palindromes (11), Parsing & Validation (7), String Building & Formatting (10), Anagrams & Character Counting (1), String Matching (KMP, Rabin-Karp, Z) (10), String Comparison & Sorting (9)
- **[03. Two Pointers](03.%20Two%20Pointers/README.md)** (54): Opposite Ends (15), Same Direction (Read-Write) (17), Two Sum & K-Sum Variants (10), Merge Two Sorted Sequences (5), Partitioning (Dutch Flag) (3), Subsequence Matching (4)
- **[04. Sliding Window](04.%20Sliding%20Window/README.md)** (57): Fixed Size Window (20), Variable Window - Longest (19), Variable Window - Shortest (2), Window with Frequency Map (12), At Most K Trick (Exactly K) (4)
- **[05. Prefix Sum](05.%20Prefix%20Sum/README.md)** (49): 1D Prefix Sum (24), Prefix Sum + Hash Map (9), 2D Prefix Sum (3), Difference Array (9), Prefix Product & Prefix XOR (4)
- **[06. Sorting](06.%20Sorting/README.md)** (32): Custom Comparator Sorting (5), Counting & Bucket Sort (10), Merge Sort Applications (5), Sort Then Scan (12)
- **[07. Matrix & Simulation](07.%20Matrix%20%26%20Simulation/README.md)** (42): Matrix Traversal (Spiral, Diagonal) (17), Matrix Rotation & Transformation (9), In-Place Matrix Marking (2), Grid Simulation (7), Process Simulation (7)
- **[08. Math & Number Theory](08.%20Math%20%26%20Number%20Theory/README.md)** (117): Digit Manipulation (26), Basic Math & Formulas (36), GCD, LCM & Primes (16), Modular Arithmetic & Fast Power (4), Combinatorics & Probability (12), Geometry (11), Game Theory (5), Randomized & Sampling (3), Big Number Arithmetic (4)
- **[09. Bit Manipulation](09.%20Bit%20Manipulation/README.md)** (33): Bit Basics & Counting (23), XOR Tricks (6), Bitmask Enumeration (2), Bitwise Operations on Ranges (2)
- **[10. Stack & Queue](10.%20Stack%20%26%20Queue/README.md)** (67): Stack Basics (8), Parentheses & Bracket Matching (9), Expression Evaluation & Calculator (8), Monotonic Stack (23), Monotonic Deque (6), Queue Basics (5), Stack Simulation (Asteroid, Path) (8)
- **[11. Binary Search](11.%20Binary%20Search/README.md)** (54): Classic Binary Search (3), Lower & Upper Bound (14), Rotated Sorted Array (4), Binary Search on Answer (24), Search in 2D Matrix (4), Peak Finding (4), Binary Search on Two Arrays (1)
- **[12. Linked List](12.%20Linked%20List/README.md)** (36): Traversal & Basic Operations (10), Reversal (4), Fast & Slow Pointers (11), Merge & Sort Lists (3), Dummy Node & Deletion (5), Add Numbers in Lists (2), Copy & Clone List (1)
- **[13. Recursion & Backtracking](13.%20Recursion%20%26%20Backtracking/README.md)** (45): Basic Recursion (6), Subsets (5), Permutations (4), Combinations & Combination Sum (4), Partitioning (6), Board & Grid Search (N-Queens, Sudoku, Word Search) (6), Generate Valid Strings (9), Divide and Conquer (5)
- **[14. Trees](14.%20Trees/README.md)** (103): DFS Traversal (Pre, In, Post) (15), BFS & Level Order (15), Tree Properties (Height, Diameter, Balance) (10), Path Sum Problems (10), Lowest Common Ancestor (6), Binary Search Tree (15), Tree Construction (10), Tree Views & Vertical Order (4), Serialize & Deserialize (3), Tree Modification (7), N-ary Tree (2), Tree as Graph (Parent Pointers, Distance K) (6)
- **[15. Tries](15.%20Tries/README.md)** (12): Basic Trie (3), Trie + DFS (3), Prefix Search & Autocomplete (5), Bitwise Trie (XOR) (1)
- **[16. Heap & Priority Queue](16.%20Heap%20%26%20Priority%20Queue/README.md)** (35): Top K Elements (5), K-Way Merge (6), Two Heaps (Median) (3), Scheduling with Heap (4), Kth Smallest or Largest (3), Greedy with Heap (14)
- **[17. Intervals](17.%20Intervals/README.md)** (15): Merge Intervals (5), Insert & Remove Interval (1), Overlapping Intervals & Meeting Rooms (4), Sweep Line (1), Interval Scheduling (4)
- **[18. Greedy](18.%20Greedy/README.md)** (76): Greedy Choice (18), Sort + Greedy (20), Jump Game & Reachability (3), Circular Greedy (Gas Station) (1), String Greedy (20), Greedy with Counting (14)
- **[19. Graphs](19.%20Graphs/README.md)** (62): Graph DFS & Connected Components (11), Grid DFS (Islands) (10), BFS Shortest Path (Unweighted) (2), Grid BFS (6), Multi-Source BFS (5), Topological Sort (11), Cycle Detection (3), Bipartite & Graph Coloring (3), Clone & Build Graph (1), Implicit Graph BFS (Word Ladder, Lock) (10)
- **[20. Advanced Graphs](20.%20Advanced%20Graphs/README.md)** (31): Union-Find (Disjoint Set) (13), Dijkstra's Algorithm (9), Bellman-Ford & Floyd-Warshall (2), 0-1 BFS (3), Minimum Spanning Tree (2), Strongly Connected Components & Bridges (1), Eulerian Path (1)
- **[21. Dynamic Programming](21.%20Dynamic%20Programming/README.md)** (135): 1D DP (Fibonacci Style) (28), Take or Skip (House Robber) (4), 0-1 Knapsack (6), Unbounded Knapsack (Coin Change) (5), Longest Increasing Subsequence (7), 2D Grid DP (15), String DP (LCS, Edit Distance) (12), Palindrome DP (4), Interval DP (9), DP on Trees (6), Bitmask DP (6), State Machine DP (Stocks) (12), Digit DP (3), Counting DP (15), Memoized DFS (3)
- **[22. Advanced Data Structures](22.%20Advanced%20Data%20Structures/README.md)** (7): Segment Tree (1), Ordered Set & TreeMap (5), Sparse Table & Binary Lifting (1)
- **[23. Design](23.%20Design/README.md)** (45): Data Structure Design (17), LRU & LFU Cache (2), Iterator Design (3), Data Stream Design (1), Randomized Design (3), System & OOP Design (15), Time-Based Design (Rate Limiter, Hit Counter) (4)
- **[24. SQL & Database](24.%20SQL%20%26%20Database/README.md)** (85): Basic Select & Filter (12), Joins (20), Aggregation & Group By (15), Subqueries & CTE (16), Window Functions (8), Date & String Functions (4), Conditional Logic (CASE) (10)
- **[25. Shell & Concurrency](25.%20Shell%20%26%20Concurrency/README.md)** (5): Shell Scripting (4), Concurrency (1)

**Columns**

- **Last Asked**: most recent LeetCode window that lists the problem for Meta.
- **30 Days / 3 Months / 6 Months / 6+ Months**: ✅ when the problem appears in that window's list.
- **Frequency**: LeetCode frequency score (0-100) in the Meta "All" list.
- **Premium**: 🔒 means LeetCode Premium problem.
- **Status**: your LeetCode status on the day this file was generated.
- **Logic / Key Idea**: one-line approach, for example "monotonic stack of indices" (you fill it in).
- **Time / Space**: time and space complexity, for example `O(n)` / `O(1)` (you fill it in).
- **Date Solved / Revisions / Confidence / Remarks**: your own tracking (you fill it in). Confidence can be Low, Medium or High.
- **Notes**: link to the problem's notes file.

Fill the hand-written columns in this file or in a chapter README. A rebuild keeps what you typed.

Within each sub-pattern, rows are sorted by most recent window, then by frequency (highest first).
